Abstract: Recent examples have shown that zero private capacity need not imply antidegradability and that two channels with zero private capacity can nevertheless transmit private information together. Existing entropic channel orders compare what a receiver and its environment can learn and thereby bound capacities. We connect these orders to the recent constructions through binary estimation, whose minimum mean-square error is governed by measured $χ^2$ divergence. A new integral representation shows how measured $χ^2$ on a qubit extension recovers quantum $χ^2$. It lets us pass from complete measured-$χ^2$ ordering to complete quantum-$χ^2$, relative-entropy, and less-noisy ordering. Zhu and Wang used a signed lift to show that their qutrit channel has zero private capacity. We show that Hermitian-smoothed measured-$χ^2$ ordering characterizes when such lifts exist, even with a quantum reference. Environmental dominance in binary estimation at every blocklength yields a finite-code reliability--secrecy bound. These results explain why complete comparison prevents activation with antidegradable helpers whereas regularized comparison alone does not. Building on that qutrit example, we establish this stability for a range of noisy Werner--Holevo channels, determine sharp private-capacity and antidegradability thresholds, and compute exact complementary capacities in a nondegradable range. By contrast, we extend Pauli half-erasure activation to all $1/2\le p<1$ and establish the same range for a new four-level family. Reference-assisted measured-$χ^2$ witnesses show why these activating constructions lack complete comparison.

</details>

Abstract: We provide the first evidence relative to a classical oracle that pseudorandom unitaries (PRUs) can exist without quantum-computable one-way functions.
  To obtain this result, we first prove that five independent random diagonal phase layers interleaved with Hadamard transforms \[ U=F_5HF_4HF_3HF_2HF_1 \] form a strong PRU with security under adaptive, controlled access to the unitary, its inverse, transpose, and complex conjugate.
  Our main result is that, when the phase layers are implemented using a classical random oracle O, the construction remains secure against uniform BQP adversaries with coherent access to suitable Boolean completions of classical-input $QMA^{PH}^{O}}$ decision problems. Such an adversary can invert (even quantum-computable) one-way functions and solve efficiently verifiable one-way puzzles defined relative to O. Our result therefore provides classical-oracle evidence that PRUs do not imply quantum-computable one-way functions, in the form of a black-box separation.
  We substantially strengthen the classical-oracle result of Kretschmer, Qian, and Tal (STOC 2025) by obtaining PRUs secure against these QMA-aided adversaries. Our construction also consists of a simple, efficient circuit that queries a random function, suggesting a route to concrete implementation via the random-oracle heuristic.

</details>

Abstract: Fault-tolerant preparation of logical states is a critical primitive for the realization of large-scale fault-tolerant quantum computing. This paper introduces SpiderCSS, a scalable compilation pipeline that generates highly-optimized, fault-tolerant state preparation circuits for arbitrary Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes. Starting from an idealized specification of a CSS state, we use the ZX-calculus and fault-equivalent rewrites to construct a diagram that prepares the given state while preserving the fault tolerance of the initial ideal ZX-diagram. From this diagram, SpiderCSS constructs a fault-tolerant CSS-state preparation circuit using a modular construction that is CNOT-minimal both within each component and also in the way these components are connected together. By only using fault-equivalent rewrites, the final circuit is provably fault-tolerant without needing expensive verification. Monte Carlo simulations across a variety of CSS codes up to distance 15 show that SpiderCSS scales efficiently and constructs circuits with substantially reduced depth and qubit footprint compared to existing scalable and heuristic methods, while simultaneously achieving an average decrease of 33.7% in logical error rate and an increase of 9.5% in acceptance rate.

</details>

### [197] [Nahm-Kirchhoff Moduli Spaces, Hyperkähler Quotient and 2D Field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03384)
*Mohamed Moussadek Maiza*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文为带边箭图与紧连通Lie群构造Nahm–Kirchhoff模空间，证明其光滑性、维数与超Kähler结构，并建立Donaldson定理的箭图类比，进而构造取值于复辛流形/超Kähler流形的2D TQFT与度量场论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Nahm方程与Kirchhoff匹配条件结合，为箭图（带边Γ）关联模空间，以推广Donaldson关于平坦联络与复辛商等同的定理，并探索Moore–Tachikawa意义下的2D TQFT及其超Kähler精细化。

Method: 在箭图边上求解Nahm方程，于内部顶点施加Kirchhoff匹配，模去在边界上平凡的规范变换；用无穷维超Kähler商构造$\mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{H}}(Γ)$；借助$G_{\mathbb{C}}/G$上的Kempf–Ness论证建立与复Nahm方程模复化规范群的复辛商$\mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{C}}(Γ)$的等同。

Result: 对连通Γ，$\mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{H}}(Γ)$是维数为$4(|E|-|Γ_{\mathrm{int}}|)\dim G$的光滑超Kähler流形；$\mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{C}}(Γ)$与边长无关，且同构于$(T^*G_{\mathbb{C}})^E$的有限维复辛商；切割-胶合给出取值于复Hamilton流形的2D TQFT；保留边长时$\mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{H}}(Γ)$构成度量配边上的度量场论，以超Kähler约化复合，纤维化于热带模空间$M_{g,n}^{\mathrm{trop}}$且复辛纤维固定；并描述内部边塌缩或趋于无穷长时的度量退化。

Conclusion: Nahm–Kirchhoff模空间在复辛层面具有函子性并给出2D TQFT，而其超Kähler精细化因边长细分改变度量而不具函子性；保留边长后可恢复函子性，得到纤维化于热带模空间的超Kähler度量场论。

Abstract: To each oriented quiver with boundary $Γ$ and compact connected Lie group $G$, we associate a Nahm--Kirchhoff moduli space $\mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{H}}(Γ)$ of solutions to Nahm's equations along the edges, subject to Kirchhoff matching at the interior vertices, modulo gauge transformations trivial on the boundary. For connected $Γ$, we prove that $\mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{H}}(Γ)$ is a smooth manifold of dimension $4(|E|-|Γ_{\mathrm{int}}|)\dim G$ carrying a hyperkähler structure obtained as an infinite-dimensional hyperkähler quotient. Using a Kempf--Ness argument on $G_{\mathbb{C}}/G$, we establish a quiver analogue of Donaldson's theorem, identifying $\mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{H}}(Γ)$ with the complex-symplectic quotient $\mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{C}}(Γ)$ of solutions to the complex Nahm equations modulo the complexified gauge group. The space $\mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{C}}(Γ)$ is independent of the edge lengths and isomorphic to a finite-dimensional complex-symplectic quotient of $(T^*G_{\mathbb{C}})^E$, whereas the hyperkähler metric depends on them. Through cutting and gluing laws, $Γ\mapsto \mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{C}}(Γ)$ defines a 2D TQFT valued in complex Hamiltonian manifolds, in the spirit of Moore--Tachikawa, while its hyperkähler refinement fails to be functorial, since subdividing an edge into sub-edges of arbitrary lengths alters the metric. Retaining the edge lengths restores functoriality: the spaces $\mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{H}}(Γ)$ assemble into a metric field theory on metric cobordisms, valued in hyperkähler manifolds composed by hyperkähler reduction, which fibres over the tropical moduli space $M_{g,n}^{\mathrm{trop}}$ with fixed complex-symplectic fibre. We also describe the degenerations of the metric as an internal edge collapses or becomes infinitely long.

</details>


### [198] [Ricci flow on manifolds with positive isotropic curvature in all dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02325)
*Simon Brendle,Raphael Tsiamis*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文将正各向同性曲率流形的分类推广到所有维度 n ≥ 5，并完善了完备非紧 κ-非塌缩古代解与梯度收缩 Ricci 孤子上的均匀正各向同性曲率分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前正各向同性曲率的分类结果受维度限制，尚未覆盖所有 n ≥ 5 的情形；同时均匀正各向同性曲率在若干重要几何对象上的分类也不完整。

Method: 证明的核心是在所有维度构造挤压锥（pinching cones），对第一作者已有结果进行精细化并推广至任意维度；借助 MATHEMATICA 寻找可行的锥族参数，最终表述自足且可手工验证，且未使用大语言模型。

Result: 建立了 n ≥ 5 任意维度的正各向同性曲率流形分类，并完成了均匀正各向同性曲率在完备非紧 κ-非塌缩古代解与梯度收缩 Ricci 孤子上的分类。

Conclusion: 该工作将正各向同性曲率分类提升到全维度，明确了相关几何流的刚性分类，并提供了可验证的锥构造框架。

Abstract: We extend the classification of manifolds with positive isotropic curvature to all dimensions $n \geq 5$. This result also completes the classification of uniformly positive isotropic curvature on complete non-compact $κ$-noncollapsed ancient solutions and gradient shrinking Ricci solitons.
  The proof involves a construction of pinching cones in all dimensions, refining and extending to all dimensions the results of the first author. We have used MATHEMATICA to find viable parameters for the cone families. The final presentation is self-contained and can be verified manually. No stage of this work involved LLMs.

</details>


### [199] [Almost Hermitian geometry and generalized Ricci flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02519)
*Anna Fino,Julieth Saavedra*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究almost-SKT（近-SKT）结构，即约化Nijenhuis张量全反对称且Bismut挠率闭的almost Hermitian流形，推广了可积情形下的SKT（pluriclosed）条件。作者引入超定椭圆性条件，得到紧流形上可容许无穷小变形的有限维性及约束Bismut–Ricci流的短时存在性；在实六维证明二分性（可积或Nijenhuis张量处处非零且范数常值、Bismut Ricci形式为零），在八维椭圆性失效，高维在开稠密集上成立Nijenhuis刚性。还刻画了全反对称性何时逐点决定度量（差一个尺度），并构造出紧六维非可积例子（含S^1×S^2×S^3、S^3×S^3），其Bismut Ricci形式为零，故为almost-CYT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SKT（pluriclosed）条件是复几何中的重要度量约束，但依赖almost complex structure的可积性。本文试图将SKT推广到非可积的almost Hermitian情形（almost-SKT），以便在更广的框架下研究其存在性、刚性与形变，并寻找约束几何流（Bismut–Ricci流）的适定性。

Method: 对固定almost complex structure，将约化Nijenhuis张量全反对称与Bismut挠率闭这两个约束视为度量的线性方程，引入超定椭圆性（overdetermined ellipticity）条件；利用椭圆性分析研究约束Bismut–Ricci流的短时存在性与可容许无穷小变形的有限维性；按实维数分类分析（六维二分性、八维椭圆性失效、高维Nijenhuis刚性）；证明全反对称性决定度量的逐点唯一性（差尺度）；并将该流与generalized Ricci flow联系，刻画almost complex structure变动时的提升障碍；最后构造紧六维非可积例子。

Result: 紧流形上椭圆性使可容许无穷小变形空间有限维，并给出约束Bismut–Ricci流的短时存在性；实六维almost-SKT满足二分性：或almost complex structure可积，或Nijenhuis张量处处非零且范数常值、Bismut Ricci形式为零，故紧非可积六维上约束流为稳态；八维椭圆性恒失效；高维在开稠密集上Nijenhuis刚性成立；任意维下，若全反对称性逐点决定度量（差尺度）且挠率闭，则almost-SKT度量在缩放意义下唯一，约束Bismut–Ricci流为显式位似解；此外构造出紧六维非可积例子（含S^1×S^2×S^3和S^3×S^3），其中度量为固定、兼容almost complex结构构成S^2族、Nijenhuis张量处处非零、特征联络非平坦且Bismut Ricci形式为零，故为almost-CYT。

Conclusion: almost-SKT为SKT提供非可积推广，其椭圆性、刚性及形变理论由维数强烈支配；六维二分性与稳态流、任意维的度量唯一性及显式位似解、以及紧六维非可积almost-CYT例子的构造，共同刻画了这一类结构的几何与流行为，并与generalized Ricci flow建立联系。

Abstract: We study almost Hermitian manifolds whose lowered Nijenhuis tensor is totally skew-symmetric and whose characteristic (Bismut) torsion is closed, the almost-SKT condition. This extends the SKT (pluriclosed) condition beyond the integrable setting. For a fixed almost complex structure, these constraints are linear in the metric. We introduce an overdetermined ellipticity condition which, on compact manifolds, makes the admissible infinitesimal deformation space finite-dimensional and yields short-time existence for a constrained Bismut--Ricci flow. In real dimension six, almost-SKT structures satisfy a dichotomy: either the almost complex structure is integrable, or the skew Nijenhuis tensor is nowhere vanishing with constant norm and the Bismut Ricci form vanishes. Hence, on compact non-integrable almost-SKT six-manifolds, the constrained Bismut--Ricci flow is stationary. In dimension eight, ellipticity always fails, while in higher dimensions Nijenhuis rigidity holds on an open dense locus. In arbitrary dimension, we characterize when total skew-symmetry of the lowered Nijenhuis tensor determines the compatible metric pointwise up to scale. On compact manifolds, with closed torsion, this implies uniqueness of the almost-SKT metric up to scaling; the constrained Bismut--Ricci flow is then an explicit homothetic solution. We also relate this flow to generalized Ricci flow and characterize the infinitesimal obstruction to lifting the evolution when the almost complex structure varies. Finally, we construct non-integrable almost-SKT structures with nowhere vanishing Nijenhuis tensor and non-flat characteristic connection on compact six-manifolds, including $S^1\times S^2\times S^3$ and $S^3\times S^3$. In these families, the metric is fixed and the compatible almost complex structures form an $S^2$-family. These structures have vanishing Bismut Ricci form and are therefore almost-CYT.

</details>


### [200] [Exact Hessian Cancellation and Quartic Nondegeneracy at the Round Circle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02797)
*Aya Ishizeki*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文计算了圆上分解Möbius能量E₁、E₂的完整法向Hessian，发现H₁=3H_E、H₂=-2H_E，由此确定Möbius不变组合F=2E₁+3E₂+2π²。证明F在圆上为零且前三阶变分消失，并通过对角化与行列式公式证明第四阶变分D⁴F(C)≥0，等号仅在Möbius族圆的正切空间取得，揭示了一个由总能量Hessian无法决定的四阶非退化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究分解Möbius能量E₁与E₂在圆处的二阶及高阶变分结构，以理解其与完整Möbius能量Hessian的关系，并识别出一个由Möbius不变性自然选出的特殊组合F，探究其四阶非退化性质。

Method: 计算E₁、E₂在圆处的完整法向Hessian；利用Hessian的精确比例关系构造Möbius不变组合F；对标量副法向变分，通过显式加权差分三对角化与闭式行列式公式分析第四变分的Fourier张量（3+1共振抵消、2+2固定和块）；对R³中任意法向变分，借助R⁴中的Möbius不变性将径向分量约化为第二副法向，并将四阶形式分解为迹、对称无迹与反对称块，验证其正性。

Result: H₁(C)=2π(|D_s|³-|D_s|)I₂，H₂(C)=-(4π/3)(|D_s|³-|D_s|)I₂，故H₁=3H_E、H₂=-2H_E；F(C)=0且前三阶导数在参数化变分空间上消失；标量副法向变分的第四变分具有有限正定块；任意法向变分下D⁴F(C)[u,u,u,u]≥0，等号恰在Möbius族圆的正切空间上。

Conclusion: 圆处存在一种四阶非退化现象，它无法仅由总能量的Hessian决定；Möbius不变的组合F在高阶变分意义下于圆处达到极小，其等号集精确刻画了Möbius族圆的正切方向。

Abstract: We study the decomposed Möbius energies $E_1$ and $E_2$ at the round circle and determine their complete normal Hessians: $H_1(C)=2π(|D_s|^3-|D_s|)I_2$ and $H_2(C)=-(4π/3)(|D_s|^3-|D_s|)I_2$. Thus $H_1(C)=3H_E(C)$ and $H_2(C)=-2H_E(C)$, where $H_E(C)$ is the Hessian of the full Möbius energy. This exact proportionality singles out the Möbius-invariant combination $F=2E_1+3E_2+2π^2$. We prove that $F(C)=0$ and that its first three derivatives vanish on the full space of parametrized variations. For scalar binormal variations, the fourth variation has a Fourier tensor with complete $3+1$ resonance cancellation and finite $2+2$ fixed-sum blocks. Their positivity follows from an explicit weighted-difference tridiagonalization and a closed determinant formula. For arbitrary normal variations in $\mathbb{R}^3$, Möbius invariance in $\mathbb{R}^4$ reduces the radial component to a second binormal direction. The resulting two-component quartic form splits into trace, symmetric-traceless, and antisymmetric blocks, which are positive on the active coordinates. Consequently, $D^4F(C)[u,u,u,u]\geq 0$ for every smooth real normal field $u$, with equality exactly on the normal tangent space of the Möbius family of round circles. This yields a fourth-order nondegeneracy phenomenon that is not determined by the total-energy Hessian alone.

</details>


### [201] [A geometric flow associated to $Q$-curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02993)
*Jiaqi Chen,Xudong Kang,Wei Yuan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了由Branson Q-曲率线性化的形式伴随所定义的四阶几何流 ∂_t g = -2J_g，在闭黎曼流形（n≥4）上建立了短时存在性、积分光滑估计、四维长时间存在与收敛性，并证明四维闭J-孤子必为平凡且度量为Bach-平坦、Q-曲率为常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lin与Yuan通过线性化Q-曲率的形式伴随引入了对称2-张量J_g，并给出其用Bach张量及辅助曲率项的显式分解；本文旨在研究与之对应的四阶几何流，以理解Q-曲率类比下Ricci流的对应理论（短时存在、长时间行为、孤子分类）。

Method: 采用双调和DeTurck规范（biharmonic DeTurck gauge）证明流的短时存在性与唯一性，并在曲率有界条件下推导全局与局部积分光滑估计；在四维情形结合一致Sobolev不等式与足够小的总L²曲率能量研究长时间存在性。

Result: 建立了短时存在唯一性与积分光滑估计；四维下在一致Sobolev不等式与全程足够小总L²曲率能量假设下证明长时间存在；在一致曲率与Sobolev界下，J的L²范数时间可积蕴含固定坐标下光滑收敛到J-平坦度量。显式Einstein与乘积解说明演化并表明小曲率能量本身不能阻止退化。正初始Yamabe常数与正总Q-曲率给出四维流一致的Yamabe下界，而高维中总Q-曲率即使在近平坦度量附近也可能下降。

Conclusion: 四维闭J-孤子均为平凡，其度量为Bach-平坦且Q-曲率为常数，孤子向量场为Killing场。

Abstract: Branson's $Q$-curvature is a natural fourth-order counterpart of scalar curvature in conformal geometry. In analogy with the classical recovery of the Ricci tensor from the linearized scalar curvature, Lin and Yuan introduced a symmetric $2$-tensor $J_g$ through the formal adjoint of the linearized $Q$-curvature and established its explicit decomposition in terms of the Bach tensor and auxiliary curvature terms. In this paper, we study the associated fourth-order geometric flow $\partial_t g = -2J_g$ on closed Riemannian manifolds of dimension $n \ge 4$. We establish short-time existence and uniqueness using a biharmonic DeTurck gauge and derive global and local integral smoothing estimates under curvature bounds. In dimension four, we prove long-time existence assuming a uniform Sobolev inequality and sufficiently small total $L^2$ curvature energy throughout the maximal interval of existence. Under uniform curvature and Sobolev bounds, time integrability of the $L^2$-norm of $J$ implies smooth convergence in fixed coordinates to a $J$-flat metric. Explicit Einstein and product solutions illustrate the evolution and show that small curvature energy alone does not prevent degeneration. Positive initial Yamabe constant and positive total $Q$-curvature give a uniform positive Yamabe lower bound throughout the smooth four-dimensional flow, whereas in higher dimensions total $Q$-curvature can decrease even near flat metrics. Finally, we prove that every closed four-dimensional $J$-soliton is trivial, its metric is Bach-flat with constant $Q$-curvature, and its soliton vector field is Killing.

</details>


### [202] [Index and nullity of minimal surfaces via representation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03023)
*Mikhail Karpukhin,Mario B. Schulz*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种表示论方法，仅凭极小曲面的拓扑与对称群即可确定其Morse指数与零维数，并通过等变比较与二面体群表示论在欧氏单位球与圆球中完成多类极小曲面的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上确定极小曲面的Morse指数与零维数通常依赖具体构造与繁琐的变分计算，缺乏仅由拓扑和对称性决定的统一途径，因此作者希望发展一种表示论框架，使这些指标可仅由拓扑与对称群推出，且独立于具体构造。

Method: 方法结合了面积与能量第二变分的等变比较（equivariant comparison），以及二面体群的表示论。通过将变分问题约化到对称群的不可约表示上，从而计算Morse指数与零维数。

Result: 在欧氏单位球中：对每个k≥3，计算了具有4k阶棱柱对称性的任意自由边界极小k-noid，以及具有8阶反棱柱对称性、亏格为1且边界连通的自由边界极小曲面的Morse指数与零维数。在圆球中：仅使用Lawson曲面第一族的一半对称群，给出了其Morse指数与零维数的另一种计算。结果均独立于曲面的具体构造。

Conclusion: 该表示论方法可仅由拓扑与对称群确定极小曲面的Morse指数与零维数，并在欧氏单位球与圆球的多类极小曲面上成功应用，结果不依赖于具体构造，显示出方法的普适性与有效性。

Abstract: We develop a representation-theoretic approach to determine the Morse index and nullity of an embedded minimal surface based only on its topology and symmetry group. The method combines an equivariant comparison between the second variations of area and energy with the representation theory of the dihedral group. In the Euclidean unit ball we compute for every $k\geq3$ the Morse index and nullity of any free boundary minimal $k$-noid with prismatic symmetry of order $4k$, and of any free boundary minimal surface of genus one with connected boundary and antiprismatic symmetry of order eight. In the round sphere we obtain an alternative computation for the Morse index and nullity for the first family of the Lawson surfaces, using only half of their symmetry group. The results are independent of any particular construction of the surfaces.

</details>


### [203] [The Calderón problem for harmonic spinors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03116)
*Carlos Valero*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明紧致带边spin流形的共形类由其调和旋量边界值（BC映射）唯一确定，前提是度量为局部共形实解析（LCRA）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带边spin流形上Dirac算子的边界数据（BC映射）能否反演内部几何，即探讨边界调和旋量信息对共形类与联络的决定性。

Method: 在MIT边界条件下定义Dirac算子的边界共轭（BC）映射；对两个LCRA度量，证明若BC映射在边界开子集上等价，则流形共形等价；并将该技术推广到扭曲Dirac算子，处理实解析酉联络。

Result: LCRA度量下BC映射在边界开子集上的等价推出流形共形等价；LCRA类包含共形Einstein流形及所有光滑曲面；维数大于2时，实解析酉联络由扭曲Dirac算子的BC映射唯一确定（至局部规范等价）。

Conclusion: 边界调和旋量值（BC映射）可完全编码内部共形几何与规范联络信息，表明边界数据在LCRA设定下具有强反演性。

Abstract: We show that the conformal class of a compact spin manifold with boundary is uniquely determined by its boundary values of harmonic spinors if the metric is locally conformal to a real-analytic metric in some coordinates. More precisely, we consider the boundary conjugation (BC) map for the Dirac operator corresponding to MIT boundary conditions, and prove that if two locally conformally real-analytic (LCRA) metrics have equivalent BC maps over an open subset of the boundary, then the two manifolds are conformally equivalent. The class of LCRA manifolds includes conformally Einstein manifolds, as well as all smooth surfaces. We also show that a real-analytic unitary connection on an LCRA manifold is uniquely determined up to local gauge equivalence by the BC map of its twisted Dirac operator in dimensions greater than 2.

</details>


### [204] [On the uniqueness of Ricci flows from certain Alexandrov spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03129)
*Laura Flower*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究从奇异初始数据出发的Ricci流唯一性，在缩放不变曲率界、非坍塌假设及截面曲率下界条件下，首次对来自（可能奇异）Reifenberg空间的Ricci流获得唯一性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ricci流唯一性问题即使初始数据光滑且光滑获得，在完备非紧情形也未被完全解决；而当初始数据非光滑时，即便紧致情形也所知甚少。本文旨在填补从奇异初始数据出发的Ricci流唯一性这一空白。

Method: 对从（可能奇异）Reifenberg空间出发的Ricci流，在缩放不变的曲率界、合适的非坍塌假设以及截面曲率下界的条件下，建立唯一性结果。

Result: 证明了在上述条件下的Ricci流唯一性；结合Simon–Topping和Lai的存在性结果，得到在任何Reifenberg一致体积非坍塌PIC2极限空间上定义光滑结构的典范方式。

Conclusion: 获得了这些极限空间上Perelman稳定性定理的微分同胚版本。

Abstract: We consider the problem of uniqueness of Ricci flows, starting from singular initial data. Even when the initial data is smooth and smoothly obtained, in the complete noncompact case uniqueness is not known in full generality. When starting from nonsmooth initial data, very little is known, even in the compact case.
  We obtain a uniqueness result for Ricci flows with scaling-invariant curvature bounds, a suitable noncollapsing assumption, and sectional curvature lower bounds, coming out of (possibly singular) Reifenberg spaces.
  As a consequence of our result, and existence results obtained by Simon--Topping and Lai, there is a canonical way to define a smooth structure on any Reifenberg uniformly volume-noncollapsed PIC2 limit space. Further, we obtain a diffeomorphic version of Perelman's stability theorem for these limit spaces.

</details>


### [205] [Twelve characterisations of Killing vector fields in Finsler geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03175)
*Ioan Bucataru,Georgeta Cretu,Luciana Daniliuc*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用Frölicher-Nijenhuis形式化框架,在Finsler流形上建立了Killing性质的十二种等价刻画,并给出其局部表达式及Killing方程的标量、一形式与张量形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无穷小等距(Killing向量场)保持Riemannian或Finslerian流形的许多典范几何结构。本文旨在识别Finsler流形中被无穷小等距所保持、且这种保持性可刻画该类向量场的几何结构。

Method: 以Frölicher-Nijenhuis形式化作为统一的几何框架,综合运用Finsler范数、球丛、接触结构与辛结构、Reeb向量场与Hamilton向量场、首次积分、齐次化近复结构以及Sasaki度量等工具,建立Killing性质的等价刻画,并推导其局部表达式。

Result: 建立了Finsler情形下Killing性质的十二种等价刻画,并得到Killing方程的多种等价形式,包括标量形式、一形式形式与张量形式。

Conclusion: 在所提出的方法中,上述各种表述均不被视为首要的,每一种都反映了Killing条件的不同几何侧面。

Abstract: It is well known that infinitesimal isometries preserve many of the canonical geometric structures of a Riemannian or Finslerian manifold. In this paper, we identify those geometric structures of a Finslerian manifold that are preserved by an infinitesimal isometry (a Killing vector field) and whose preservation characterises such fields. Using the Frölicher-Nijenhuis formalism as a unified geometric framework, we establish twelve equivalent characterisations of the Killing property in the Finslerian setting, utilizing the Finsler norm, the sphere bundle, contact and symplectic structures, Reeb and Hamiltonian vector fields, first integrals, the homogenized almost complex structure, and the Sasaki metrics. Furthermore, we derive the local expressions of these characterisations, yielding several equivalent forms of the Killing equations, including scalar, one-form and tensorial formulations. In our approach, none of these formulations is regarded as primary; each reflects a different geometric aspect of the Killing condition.

</details>


### [206] [Cyclic $L_3$ Models and Holomorphic Reduction in Heterotic $G_2$ Deformation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03301)
*Bram Brongers*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了异质 $G_2$ 超势变分形变理论在无挠标准嵌入附近、至 $\alpha'$ 一阶的有限循环 $L_3$ 表示（括号在三次以上为零）。辅助场给出移位余切 Hamilton 量，消去后恢复物理泛函；在平坦七维环面上该泛函在物理旋量坐标下有非零五次系数。对 $Y=S^1\times X$（$X$ 为 Calabi–Yau 三fold），限制到水平规范与 gerbe 数据的乘积轨迹，泛函化为 $\frac L4\operatorname{Im}\int_X(H+i\,d\omega)\wedge\Omega$，诱导 $SU(3)$ 结构选出 Hermitian 切 Courant 子代数胚，其复化中的横截迷向提升编码全纯联络、弦方程等，Taylor 括号构成 $L_3$ 代数且弦部分与已知全纯异质代数一致。最后证明该全纯理论是乘积变分理论有限可缩稳定化的特征 Hamilton 约化，商捕获全纯方程，而共形平衡、规范本原性与领头通量容许性是实代表上的附加条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质 $G_2$ 超势变分形变理论的数学结构尚缺乏有限阶（有限元）$L_\infty$ 表述；作者希望构造一个括号在三次以上消失的有限循环 $L_3$ 表示，并将乘积情形的全纯理论表述为特征 Hamilton 约化，从而统一变分泛函、弦方程与全纯异质代数。

Method: 在无挠标准嵌入附近、至 $\alpha'$ 一阶，构造异质 $G_2$ 超势变分理论的有限循环 $L_3$ 表示。对逆余标架、旋量导数、通量及诱导切联络引入辅助场，得到移位余切 Hamilton 量（次数至多四）；消去辅助场恢复物理泛函。对 $Y=S^1\times X$（$X$ 为 Calabi–Yau 三fold），限制到水平规范与 gerbe 数据的乘积轨迹，利用诱导 $SU(3)$ 结构选出 Hermitian 切 Courant 子代数胚，并通过复化中的横截迷向提升编码全纯联络、弦方程、诱导切联络、加权典范线与 gerbe 层级。进一步以有限可缩稳定化的特征 Hamilton 约化导出全纯理论。

Result: 得到有限循环 $L_3$ 表示；辅助场消去后恢复物理泛函，在平坦七维环面上该泛函在局部物理旋量坐标下有非零五次 Taylor 系数。乘积情形泛函约化为 $\frac L4\operatorname{Im}\int_X(H+i\,d\omega)\wedge\Omega$，其中 $L$ 为圆周长度、$\Omega=e^{-2\Phi}\Psi$ 为加权复体积。横截迷向提升的 Taylor 括号构成 $L_3$ 代数，其弦部分与已知全纯异质代数一致。全纯理论可由乘积变分理论的有限可缩稳定化的特征 Hamilton 约化在每一上链次数上得到；商捕获全纯方程，而共形平衡、规范本原性与领头通量容许性是实代表上的附加条件。

Conclusion: 异质 $G_2$ 超势变分理论在至 $\alpha'$ 一阶可由有限循环 $L_3$ 代数描述，且乘积 $S^1\times X$ 情形的全纯理论是有限可缩稳定化的特征 Hamilton 约化；商仅捕获全纯方程，共形平衡、规范本原性与领头通量容许性需作为实代表上的附加条件施加。

Abstract: We construct a finite cyclic $L_3$ presentation (that is, a cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra with brackets vanishing above arity three) of the variational deformation theory defined by the heterotic $G_2$ superpotential near a torsion-free standard embedding, to first order in $α'$. Auxiliary fields for the inverse coframe, spinor derivative, flux and induced tangent connection give a shifted-cotangent Hamiltonian of degree at most four. Eliminating the auxiliary fields recovers the physical functional. On the flat seven-torus, this functional has a nonzero quintic Taylor coefficient in local physical spinor coordinates.
  For $Y=S^1\times X$, with $X$ a Calabi--Yau threefold, we restrict to the product locus with horizontal gauge and gerbe data. The functional reduces to $
  \frac L4\operatorname{Im}\int_X(H+i\,dω)\wedgeΩ, $
where $L$ is the circle length and $Ω=e^{-2Φ}Ψ$ is the weighted complex volume. The induced $SU(3)$ structure selects a Hermitian tangent Courant subalgebroid. Transverse isotropic lifts in its complexification encode the holomorphic connection and string equations, together with the induced tangent connection, weighted canonical line and gerbe hierarchy. Their Taylor brackets form an $L_3$ algebra whose string part agrees with the known holomorphic heterotic algebra.
  We then show that this holomorphic theory is obtained by characteristic Hamiltonian reduction of a finite contractible stabilization of the product variational theory, in every cochain degree. While the quotient captures the holomorphic equations, conformal balance, gauge primitivity and leading-flux admissibility are additional conditions on a real representative.

</details>


### [207] [Positive sectional curvature on the Kervaire nine-sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03298)
*Zhiqi Chen,Junna Ni,Hui Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文借助Deng–Hu–Zhang的compatible-disk定理，在Kervaire九维球面上构造了正截面曲率度量，并给出相应的曲率下界与Dirac指标障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在奇异光滑结构的九维球面（Kervaire球面等）上建立正截面曲率度量，并厘清光滑九维球面各类型在正截面曲率与非负截面曲率上的存在性与障碍。

Method: 利用compatible-disk定理；构造光滑的共轭等变黏贴映射，其twist恰为Milnor–Munkres交换子；通过稳定化clutching数据将该球面识别为五维球面上两个相切圆盘丛的plumbing的边界；描述marked quotient度量，并用固定联络的曲率范数给出正下界；用实Dirac指标作障碍。

Result: 在Kervaire九维球面上得到正截面曲率；给出以固定联络曲率范数表示的正曲率下界；实Dirac指标排除了八种光滑九维球面类型中四种上的偶数非负截面曲率；构造覆盖标准型与Kervaire型；其余两种指标为零的类型具有正标量曲率，但其正截面曲率存在性未定。

Conclusion: 该工作将compatible-disk技术推广至Kervaire九维球面，得到正截面曲率度量及曲率下界，并以Dirac指标给出非负截面曲率的障碍，同时明确留下两类指标为零球面的正截面曲率问题。

Abstract: Using the compatible-disk theorem of Deng, Hu, and Zhang, we obtain positive sectional curvature on the Kervaire nine-sphere. We construct a smooth conjugation-equivariant attaching map whose twist is exactly a Milnor--Munkres commutator. Its stabilized clutching data identify the resulting sphere with the boundary of the plumbing of two tangent disk bundles over the five-sphere. We describe the marked quotient metrics and give a positive lower bound in terms of the curvature norms of a fixed connection. The real Dirac index excludes even nonnegative sectional curvature on four of the eight smooth nine-sphere types. The construction covers the standard and Kervaire types; the remaining two index-zero types admit positive scalar curvature, but their positive-sectional-curvature existence is not decided here.

</details>

Abstract: We construct Riemannian metrics of strictly positive sectional curvature on at least 508 pairwise nondiffeomorphic exotic fifteen-spheres, without distinguishing orientations. Abstract: We give a detailed and complete algebraic proof of Simons'
  Riemannian holonomy theorem by a single strong induction on the dimension of the linear span of the curvature orbit. The proof uses the construction of flats and root centralizers, but replaces the detailed analysis of the common zero-weight space, the most technical core of previous algebraic
  proofs, by a trace-vanishing lemma. The lemma applies to curvature operators contained in an ideal that annihilates the curvature module. Restriction to a common geodesic subspace produces a proper invariant kernel. Induction makes this kernel fixed by the holonomy action, after which the trace lemma shows that the root centralizers span the ambient space. Their intersection recovers the maximal flat, and irreducibility completes the proof. For the completeness we also include the detailed derivation of the local symmetry of the underlying Riemannian manifold
  from the algebraic theorem.

Abstract: Let $M^n$, $n\ge3$, be the smooth, connected, complete, noncompact boundary of a closed convex set with nonempty interior in $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$. We prove that its normalized scalar-curvature integral at infinity is at most $4πω_{n-2}(1-\operatorname{AVR}(M))$, except when, up to an ambient rigid motion, $M$ is the product of a smooth closed convex surface in $\mathbb{R}^3$ and $\mathbb{R}^{n-2}$. These exceptional products have zero asymptotic volume ratio and limit $8πω_{n-2}$. We also characterize the equality case for the $4πω_{n-2}(1-\operatorname{AVR}(M))$ bound. The proof uses exterior tube volumes together with the cone Steiner and Gauss--Bonnet formulas. An elementary comparison of intrinsic and Euclidean radial distances proves existence of the intrinsic limit and identifies its exact value from the cone coefficients. The same conclusions extend to orientable convex submanifolds of arbitrary codimension

Abstract: Given a Finsler manifold $(M,F)$ and suitable nested regions $B_1\subset B_2\subset B_3$, with $M\setminus B_1$ connected and non-contractible, as in the model case of concentric balls in $\mathbb R^n$, $n\geq2$, we consider conformal deformations $\varphi_λF$ which can be chosen so as to leave $F$ unchanged outside $B_3$ and grow on $B_2\setminus B_1$. This growth creates an energy barrier, diverging with $λ$, that low-energy curves cannot cross. The minimax families can therefore be chosen in the path space of $M\setminus B_1$, which contains compact subsets of arbitrarily large category. Consequently, for every $m\in\mathbb N$ and all sufficiently large $λ$, there exist at least $m$ geodesics joining two prescribed points, all avoiding $B_1$. This extends to the Finsler setting an idea of Capozzi, Fortunato and Greco. The same scheme applies to Randers metrics $α+β$, with $α$ deformed and $β$ unchanged, as well as to fixed-energy trajectories of non-relativistic electromagnetic systems and relativistic Lorentz-force trajectories, and light rays in spacetimes endowed with a causal Killing field. In the non-relativistic case only the scalar potential is perturbed, while the magnetic field is preserved. When the Killing field is not everywhere timelike, the Fermat principle yields a possibly singular Randers--Kropina metric on a spacelike hypersurface. Under a natural admissible category condition, combining localized barriers with Randers approximation gives $m$ Randers--Kropina geodesics with pairwise distinct lengths, equivalently $m$ future-pointing light rays with pairwise distinct arrival times.

</details>

Abstract: The generalization of the Kähler metric in the complex Finsler setting admits four natural versions. Among these, the strongly Kähler--Finsler condition is known to coincide with one of the intermediate conditions. In this paper, we establish the equivalence between the weakly Kähler--Finsler condition and the remaining intermediate condition. Moreover, we obtain new necessary and sufficient conditions for a weakly Kähler--Finsler metric to be strongly Kähler--Finsler. Abstract: We introduce a \emph{global} frequency function for harmonic functions on rotationally symmetric Cartan-Hadamard manifolds. In real hyperbolic space, we also identify a different frequency function associated with the normalized spherical $L^2$-mass of a hyperbolic harmonic function and prove its monotonicity. As a consequence, we obtain a logarithmic convexity theorem for the normalized spherical $L^2$-mass and a hyperbolic analog of the classical three-sphere theorem.

</details>

### [217] [Graded twisted reflection matrices of GL-type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02463)
*D. Algethami,A. Mudrov*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文对 $\mathbb{Z}_2$-分次扭曲反射方程在基本 $GL$ 型 R-矩阵与任意宇称序列下的常数数值解进行了完整分类，无需假设偶性或可逆性，并由此刻画奇异解与可逆解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反射方程的常数解在可积系统与量子群表示论中具有基础意义，而分次（$\mathbb{Z}_2$-graded）情形下的系统分类尚不完整；既有工作多假设解为偶或可逆，且部分结论仅为猜想。本文旨在去掉这些限制，给出统一分类。

Method: 作者针对该类反射方程建立了四指标局域性原理（four-index locality principle），并以此为基础，在不假设偶性与可逆性的前提下对基本 $GL$ 型 R-矩阵与任意宇称序列的常数数值解进行分类。

Result: 给出了奇异解与可逆解的完整判定与分类；恢复了此前对最小对称分次所猜想的矩阵；证明偶可逆解与近期分次 Satake 图分类相容；并表明全支撑下三角非偶族的经典稳定化子是一个 Lie 超代数，其偶部为电 Lie 代数（electrical Lie algebra）。

Conclusion: 四指标局域性原理提供了分次扭曲反射方程常数解分类的有效工具，所得分类既推广了已有结果、验证了猜想，又建立了与分次 Satake 图分类及电 Lie 代数的联系。

Abstract: We classify constant numerical solutions of the $\Z_2$-graded twisted reflection equation for the fundamental $GL$-type R-matrix and an arbitrary parity sequence, without assuming evenness or invertibility. The classification is based on a four-index locality principle established for this type of reflection equation. We determine the singular and invertible solutions and recover previously conjectured matrices for the minimal symmetric grading. Even invertible solutions comply with the recent classification of graded Satake diagrams. The classical stabilizer of a full-support lower-triangular non-even family is a Lie superalgebra whose even part is an electrical Lie algebra.

</details>


### [218] [The double covering of compact quantum group $SO_q(4)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02929)
*Bipul Saurabh*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文证明 ℤ₂ 是 SU_q(2)×SU_q(2) 的量子正规子群，且商群同构于 SO_q(4)，从而 SU_q(2)×SU_q(2) 是 SO_q(4) 的双覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典情形下 SU(2)×SU(2) 是 SO(4) 的双覆盖，本文旨在将这一结构推广到量子群层面，建立量子版本的对应关系。

Method: 通过在量子群框架内构造 SU_q(2)×SU_q(2) 中的 ℤ₂ 量子正规子群，并计算相应商群、证明其与 SO_q(4) 同构。

Result: ℤ₂ 确为 SU_q(2)×SU_q(2) 的量子正规子群，商群同构于 SO_q(4)，故 SU_q(2)×SU_q(2) 构成 SO_q(4) 的双覆盖。

Conclusion: 量子群 SU_q(2)×SU_q(2) 与 SO_q(4) 之间存在与经典情形对应的双覆盖关系。

Abstract: We show that $\mathbb{Z}_2$ is a quantum normal subgroup of $SU_q(2)\times SU_q(2)$ and that the quotient group is isomorphic to $SO_q(4)$, thereby establishing that $SU_q(2)\times SU_q(2)$ is a double cover of $SO_q(4)$.

</details>

### [219] [Homotopy Self-Equivalences of Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02244)
*Mehmetcik Pamuk*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文将 Hambleton–Kreck 关于 4 维流形基于自等价群 $\operatorname{Aut}_\bullet(M)$ 的交换辫框架推广到 $2n$ 或 $2n-1$ 维 $BO\langle n+1\rangle$-流形，在结构映射经由 Postnikov $n$-型分解 (F) 与单值性平凡 (T) 两条假设下给出计算方法，并应用于球面乘积、连通和等实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于自等价群 $\operatorname{Aut}_\bullet(M)$ 是流形分类中的自然不变量，Hambleton–Kreck 的交换辫为 4 维流形提供了系统计算手段；作者希望把这一框架拓展到更高维的 $BO\langle n+1\rangle$-流形，并借此比较同伦分类与微分同胚分类的差异。

Method: 推广 Hambleton–Kreck 交换辫至 $2n$/ $2n-1$ 维 $BO\langle n+1\rangle$-流形，附加假设 (F) 结构映射经 Postnikov $n$-型分解、(T) 相关单值性平凡（稳定平行化时均成立）；分析乘积与球面连通和；对 $S^n\times S^n$ 用 Whitehead 乘积论证直接验证相关相交形式的等距群（$n$ 奇为 $Sp_2(\mathbb{Z})=SL_2(\mathbb{Z})$，$n$ 偶为 $(\mathbb{Z}/2)^2$），代入辫得到短正合列 $0\to K\to\operatorname{Aut}_\bullet(M)\to Q\to0$，其中 $K$ 为基于球面不稳定同伦群的配边论核；逐一计算 $S^1\times S^{m-1}$、$S^3\times S^3$、$r$ 个 $S^3\times S^3$ 的连通和、$S^3\times S^4$、$S^4\times S^4$ 及 $(S^3\times S^5)\#(S^3\times S^5)$。

Result: 对 $S^n\times S^n$，$Q$ 分别为 $Sp_2(\mathbb{Z})=SL_2(\mathbb{Z})$（$n$ 奇）与 $(\mathbb{Z}/2)^2$（$n$ 偶）；$S^1\times S^{m-1}$ 给出相应短正合列；$S^3\times S^3$ 情形序列分裂为直积；$r$ 个 $S^3\times S^3$ 连通和中置换各求和项在 $K$ 上非平凡作用；$S^3\times S^4$ 完成计算；$S^4\times S^4$ 中 $Q$ 完全确定而 $K$ 仅为猜想；$(S^3\times S^5)\#(S^3\times S^5)$ 方法推进大部分但未完全闭合。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将 Hambleton–Kreck 交换辫推广到高维 $BO\langle n+1\rangle$-流形，并在多类球面乘积与连通和上给出 $\operatorname{Aut}_\bullet(M)$ 的精确或部分描述；通过与光滑结构集的比较，揭示了同伦分类与微分同胚分类在某些情形下可以表现出显著不同的行为。

Abstract: Let $M$ be a closed, oriented manifold. The group $\operatorname{Aut}_\bullet(M)$ of based self-equivalences of $M$ is a natural invariant in manifold classification, and Hambleton and Kreck's commutative braid gives a systematic way to compute it for $4$-manifolds. We extend their framework to $BO\langle n+1\rangle$-manifolds of dimension $2n$ or $2n-1$, under two hypotheses: (F), that the structure map factors through the Postnikov $n$-type, and (T), triviality of the relevant monodromy. Both hold whenever $M$ is stably parallelizable, which is the case in every example we treat. We then discuss products and connected sums of spheres. For $S^n\times S^n$, the isometry group of the relevant intersection form is the symplectic group $Sp_2(\mathbb{Z})=SL_2(\mathbb{Z})$ when $n$ is odd, and the finite group $(\mathbb{Z}/2)^2$ when $n$ is even, which we verify directly with a Whitehead-product argument. Feeding this into the braid gives, in each case, a short exact sequence $0\to K\to\operatorname{Aut}_\bullet(M)\to Q\to0$, with $Q$ this same isometry group and $K$ a bordism-theoretic kernel built from unstable homotopy groups of spheres. We work this out for $S^1\times S^{m-1}$; for $S^3\times S^3$, where the sequence splits as a direct product; for connected sums of $r$ copies of $S^3\times S^3$, where permuting the summands acts non-trivially on $K$ instead; for $S^3\times S^4$; and for $S^4\times S^4$, where $Q$ is fully determined but $K$ is only conjectured. We also discuss the connected sum $(S^3\times S^5)$ \connsum $(S^3\times S^5)$, where the same method gets most of the way to an answer without quite closing it. Throughout, comparing with the smooth structure set shows how differently homotopy and diffeomorphism classification can behave.

</details>


### [220] [Regular bundles on orbifolds II: ranks and obstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02555)
*Enrique Becerra,Ernesto Lupercio*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 研究 orbispace 上正则复丛的秩界与稳定正则指数，给出稳定子阶≤2时五维、六维情况的锐界，并刻划循环 gerbe 的稳定正则指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则复丛的纤维是稳定子正则表示的整数倍，其秩的刻画与经典向量丛理论不同，需要针对轨道空间（orbi-空间/轨道流形）建立锐利的秩界，并统一处理不同维数、稳定子阶数与光滑/拓扑情形。

Method: 利用正则表示的倍数结构、稳定子阶数约束与积分 Adams 乘子；计算循环 gerbe 的稳定正则指数为各特征扭曲指数的最小公倍数；结合外幂表示与约化类条件构造界并证锐。

Result: 稳定子阶≤2时，五维拟紧 orbispace 锐界为16，闭定向光滑六维 orbifold 亦为16，闭定向光滑五维 orbifold 为4；秩16可在自由轨迹稠密且非自由轨迹正则秩最小为4时达到；循环 gerbe 稳定正则指数等于特征扭曲指数的最小公倍数；$S^4\times B\mu_2$ 上正则秩4丛的外幂表达式最小正秩为32。

Conclusion: 正则复丛秩界在拓扑与光滑轨道情形下存在显著差异（16与4），积分 Adams 乘子给出与维数和稳定子阶数相关的显式界，循环 gerbe 的稳定正则指数可由特征扭曲指数完全确定。

Abstract: A regular complex bundle on an orbispace has fibres that are positive multiples of the stabilizers' regular representations. For stabilizers of order at most two, the sharp uniform rank bound in dimension five is $16$ for paracompact orbispaces and $4$ for closed oriented smooth orbifolds. Rank $16$ occurs with dense free locus and minimum regular rank $4$ on the nonfree locus. We also prove the sharp bound $16$ for closed oriented smooth six-orbifolds. We compute the stable regular index of cyclic gerbes over finite connected CW complexes as the least common multiple of the indices of their character twists. For paracompact orbispaces, optimal integral Adams multipliers give explicit rank bounds in terms of dimension and stabilizer orders. The least positive rank of an integral exterior-power expression with regular fibre and zero reduced class for every regular rank-four bundle on $S^4\times Bμ_2$ is $32$.

</details>


### [221] [The zeroth stable homotopy groups of motivic spheres over the integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03069)
*Oliver Röndigs*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文确定了整数Morel-Voevodsky动机稳定同伦范畴中动机球谱的第零个整Milnor-Witt茎，其零权分量为整数上的Grothendieck-Witt环；同时刻画了多种动机谱的胞性并给出混合特征Dedekind域上η可逆动机球谱的绝对纯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机稳定同伦论中，动机球谱的Milnor-Witt茎是理解同伦群结构、与代数K理论及Hermitian K理论联系的核心不变量。已有工作多针对特定基域或局部情形，整数情形（即Bℤ或更一般的混合特征基环）的第零个整Milnor-Witt茎尚缺乏完整确定，且动机谱的胞性及绝对纯性等结构性工具在任意域与混合特征Dedekind域上尚未充分建立。

Method: 在Morel-Voevodsky动机稳定同伦范畴中研究整数上的动机球谱𝕊，通过分析其第零个整Milnor-Witt茎，识别零权分量为Grothendieck-Witt环GW(ℤ)。技术手段包括：在任意域上证明若干动机谱（特别地整动机Eilenberg-MacLane谱Hℤ）的胞性（cellularity），并利用η可逆动机球谱在混合特征Dedekind域上的绝对纯性（absolute purity）结果作为计算与结构性论证的工具。

Result: 确定了整数上动机球谱的第零个整Milnor-Witt茎，其权重零分量为Grothendieck-Witt环。证明了任意域上包括整动机Eilenberg-MacLane谱Hℤ在内的多种动机谱的胞性。建立了混合特征Dedekind域上η可逆动机球谱的绝对纯性。

Conclusion: 该工作将动机球谱Milnor-Witt茎的计算推广至整数情形，权重零分量由Grothendieck-Witt环给出，深化了动机同伦论与Hermitian K理论的联系；所建立的胞性与绝对纯性结果具有独立价值，为在任意域及混合特征基环上进一步计算动机同伦群提供了技术基础。abstract

Abstract: The main result determines the zeroth integral Milnor-Witt stem of the motivic sphere spectrum in the Morel-Voevodsky motivic stable homotopy category of the integers. The component in weight zero is the Grothendieck-Witt ring of nondegenerate symmetric bilinear forms over the integers. Along the way, cellularity of various motivic spectra, including the integral motivic Eilenberg-MacLane spectrum, is established over arbitrary fields, as well as an absolute purity result for the η-inverted motivic sphere spectrum over Dedekind domains of mixed characteristic.

</details>


### [222] [Equivariant Poincaré polynomial of type B Wonderful Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03138)
*Roberto Pagaria,Tommaso Scognamiglio*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 研究超八面体群在B型反射arrangement的极小De Concini-Procesi模型上同调上的作用，用A型等变Poincaré多项式生成函数及B型等变约化特征级数表出，并给出等变关联代数中的一般反演公式及B型组合恒等式与显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画B型反射arrangement对应极小De Concini-Procesi wonderful模型的等变上同调结构，并推广A型中已知的等变特征级数与组合恒等式结果到B型。

Method: 利用等变关联代数中联系等变Chow函数与超约化特征函数的一般反演公式；将其应用于partition lattice与signed partition lattice。

Result: 将等变Poincaré多项式生成函数表示为A型级数与B型等变约化特征级数的组合；由反演公式恢复Getzler的A型compositional identity并建立其B型类比；给出B型等变特征级数的显式plethystic公式与无穷乘积公式。

Conclusion: 该反演公式统一处理A型与B型的等变特征级数，得到B型wonderful模型等变Poincaré多项式的生成函数表达及若干显式组合公式。

Abstract: We study the actions of hyperoctahedral groups on the cohomology of the minimal De Concini-Procesi wonderful models associated with reflection arrangements of type $B$. We express the generating series of their equivariant Poincaré polynomials in terms of the corresponding type $A$ series and the equivariant reduced characteristic series of type $B$. The key ingredient is a general inversion formula in equivariant incidence algebras relating equivariant Chow functions and super-reduced characteristic functions. Applying this formula to partition and signed partition lattices, we recover Getzler's compositional identity in type $A$ and establish its type $B$ analogue. We also obtain explicit plethystic and infinite-product formulas for the equivariant characteristic series of type $B$.

</details>


### [223] [Eilenberg-MacLane spaces in A-homotopy theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03497)
*Georg Wille*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究无向图的离散同伦论，构造实现任意给定A-同伦群的图，并证明离散Blakers-Massey定理，构建部分Postnikov系统与Whitehead塔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Barcelo、Kramer、Laubenbacher和Weaver提出了图的A-同伦群，但尚不清楚哪些群的序列可由图的A-同伦群实现，以及离散同伦论中是否具备经典同伦论的核心工具（如Blakers-Massey定理、Postnikov系统、Whitehead塔）。本文旨在回答这些问题，推动图的离散同伦论发展。

Method: 作者对每个度数n和每个群G（n≥2时G为Abel群）构造一个图，使其第n个A-同伦群同构于G而其余A-同伦群全部消失；将相对A-同伦群与经典相对同伦群进行等同识别，并证明离散版的Blakers-Massey定理；在此基础上构造部分Postnikov系统和完整Whitehead塔。

Result: 证明了：对任意无限群序列G_1,G_2,...（i≥2时G_i为Abel群），存在一个图X使得其第i个A-同伦群同构于G_i对所有i成立；相对A-同伦群可等同于经典相对同伦群；离散Blakers-Massey定理成立；并成功构造了部分Postnikov系统和完整Whitehead塔。

Conclusion: 该工作表明图的A-同伦群具有充分的表达力，可实现在给定约束下的任意群序列，并且离散同伦论能够承载经典同伦论的核心结构（相对同伦、Blakers-Massey、Postnikov系统、Whitehead塔），从而为图的离散同伦论奠定了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper we study a discrete homotopy theory for undirected graphs. More precisely, we consider $A$-homotopy groups, first defined in the work of Barcelo, Kramer, Laubenbacher and Weaver. For every degree $n$ and every group $G$ (Abelian if $n\geq 2$), we construct a graph whose $n$-th $A$-homotopy group is isomorphic to $G$ and whose other $A$-homotopy groups all vanish. Using these, we show that for every infinite sequence of groups $G_1,G_2,\ldots$, with $G_i$ Abelian for $i\geq 2$, there is a graph $X$ such that the $i$-th $A$-homotopy group of $X$ is isomorphic to $G_i$ for all $i$. Along the way, we identify relative $A$-homotopy groups with relative classical homotopy groups, and prove a discrete version of the Blakers-Massey theorem. We then construct partial Postnikov systems and (full) Whitehead towers.

</details>


### [224] [The Squire complex and its extensions revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03608)
*Elton Pasku*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文为Squier复形 $\mathcal{D}(\cP)$ 及其若干扩展的CW复形维数假设提供严格证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Squier复形 $\mathcal{D}(\cP)$ 及其扩展在 \cite{LDHM2}、\cite{SES}、\cite{EP} 等文献中被广泛使用，但这些工作依赖于 $\mathcal{D}(\cP)$ 及其扩展分别为2维和3维CW复形这一前提，却未给出证明。

Method: 通过组合拓扑方法，验证 $\mathcal{D}(\cP)$ 及 \cite{LDHM2} 与 \cite{SES} 中扩展的CW复形结构，并确定其维数。

Result: 证明了 $\mathcal{D}(\cP)$ 是2维CW复形，以及 \cite{LDHM2} 和 \cite{SES} 中的扩展分别为相应的3维CW复形。

Conclusion: 填补了已有文献中关于Squier复形及其扩展CW复形维数缺乏证明的空白，为相关结果提供了严格基础。

Abstract: The Squier complex $\mathcal{D}(\cP)$ has been defined in \cite{LDHM1} as a combinatorial 2-complex. Also several extensions of $\mathcal{D}(\cP)$ are defined and used extensively in \cite{LDHM2}, \cite{SES} and \cite{EP}. Since the results obtained in those papers rely on the assumption that $\mathcal{D}(\cP)$ and its extensions in \cite{LDHM2} and \cite{SES} are CW-complexes of dimensions 2 and 3, respectively, and no proofs are given to support these preconditions, we provide the respective proofs in the present paper.

</details>

### [225] [On the Cauchy Problem for the Sawada-Kotera Equation: Soliton resolution conjecture, Painlevé transcendents and asymptotic stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03097)
*Zheng-Kang Huang,Shou-Fu Tian*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究含连续谱与有限离散谱的可容许初值下 Sawada-Kotera (SK) 方程的长时渐近行为，证明了孤子分解猜想，给出了由 F-XVIII Painlevé 超越函数支配的渐近式，并建立了 N-孤子解的渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往 SK 方程的长时渐近理论仅限于纯连续谱情形，缺乏对孤子与辐射共存的刻画，因此需要将长时渐近理论推广至同时包含连续谱与有限离散谱的可容许初值。

Method: 构造新的亚纯 Riemann-Hilbert 框架，通过完整六点极点轨道纳入离散谱，同时保持余子式解析性与谱原点处的奇异结构；结合按轨道、按区域依赖的极点约化机制与 \bar{\partial}-非线性最速下降法，选取各区域贡献的孤子并将其与连续辐射分离；利用正则修正 SK 问题与 Miura 映射处理谱原点处合并的驻点。

Result: 在不同时空区域导出了 SK 方程的长时渐近式；证明了孤子分解猜想；渐近行为由 F-XVIII Painlevé 超越函数支配；建立了 N-孤子解的渐近稳定性；实现了孤子与辐射共存情形的长时渐近刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作将 SK 方程的长时渐近理论从纯连续谱推广到孤子与辐射共存的一般情形，所提出的亚纯 Riemann-Hilbert 与轨道极点约化框架为解决同类可积系统的长时渐近问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: We derive long-time asymptotics in different space-time regions for the Sawada-Kotera (SK) equation with admissible data involving continuous spectrum and finite discrete spectrum. Our result proves the soliton resolution conjecture, yields the asymptotics governed by the F-XVIII Painlevé transcendent, and establishes the asymptotic stability of \(N\)-soliton solutions. More importantly, this extends the long-time asymptotic theory to the coexistence of solitons and radiation, whereas previous results were restricted to the purely continuous spectrum. One key ingredient in our argument is a new meromorphic Riemann-Hilbert framework that incorporates the discrete spectrum through complete six-point pole orbits while preserving cofactor analyticity and the singular structure at the spectral origin. A second ingredient is the combination of an orbitwise, region-dependent pole reduction mechanism and the \(\bar{\partial}\)-nonlinear steepest descent method, which allows us to select the solitons contributing in each region and separate them from the continuous radiation. Our framework also resolves the coalescing stationary points at the singular spectral origin through the regular modified SK problem and the Miura map.

</details>


### [226] [Noncommutative Marcus-Ree inequality and Erdős channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02294)
*Repana Devendra,Chaman Kumar Sahu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 Marcus–Ree 关于双随机矩阵的 Frobenius 范数不等式推广到量子信道，定义 Erdős 信道，并系统研究其结构、分类与性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双随机矩阵与非负矩阵理论中，Marcus 和 Ree (1959) 证明了双随机矩阵的平方 Frobenius 范数不超过其最大对角和。本文旨在寻找该经典结果的量子（非交换）类比，即对 unital 量子信道的相应不等式，并刻画取等情形（Erdős 信道）以理解量子信道与矩阵的对应结构。

Method: 1) 对 M_d 上的 mixed unitary 信道证明 Frobenius 范数不等式的量子版本，并给出一般 unital 信道的反例。2) 定义 Erdős 信道（取等信道）。3) 证明到任意 unital C*-子代数的保迹条件期望是 Erdős 信道，且在相同 Choi rank 的信道中局部孤立。4) 引入量子 RCDS 信道（所有 support 置换具有相同对角和的双随机矩阵的非交换类比），建立结构定理并刻画量子 RCDS Erdős 信道。5) 给出 Choi rank 2 的 Erdős 信道的显式形式（至多相差 unitary 等价），并证明存在不可数多个 unitary 等价类。6) 刻画任意 Choi rank 的 mixed Weyl-unitary 信道中的 Erdős 信道。7) 对 qubit 情形证明恰有四个 unitary 等价类。

Result: 证明了 unital 信道 Frobenius 范数不等式的量子版本并对 mixed unitary 信道成立、一般 unital 信道不成立；刻画的 Erdős 信道包括：保迹条件期望、量子 RCDS 信道、Choi rank 2 的显式形式、mixed Weyl-unitary 信道。Choi rank 2 的 Erdős 信道存在不可数多个 unitary 等价类（与经典情形不同）。qubit 情形恰有四个 unitary 等价类，由恒等信道和 Pauli 信道的均匀平均表示。对角信道情形可恢复 Marcus–Ree 不等式、RCDS 双随机矩阵结构及 Erdős 刻画。

Conclusion: 本文建立了量子版本的 Marcus–Ree 不等式与 Erdős 信道理论，系统刻画了多类 Erdős 信道及其等价类，揭示了量子情形与经典情形的重要差异（如不可数多个等价类），并说明经典结果可作为对角信道特例被统一纳入该框架。

Abstract: Unital quantum channels are the noncommutative analogues of doubly stochastic matrices. In 1959, Marcus and Ree showed that the squared Frobenius norm of a doubly stochastic matrix is at most its maximal diagonal sum. We prove a quantum version of this inequality for mixed unitary channels on $\mathbb{M}_d$ and give an example showing that it fails for general unital channels. As in the classical case, we call the channels attaining equality as Erdős channels.
  We show that the trace-preserving conditional expectation onto any unital $C^*$-subalgebra of $\mathbb{M}_d$ is an Erdős channel. It is also locally isolated among channels of the same Choi rank. Next, we introduce quantum RCDS channels. These are the noncommutative analogue of doubly stochastic matrices whose supported permutations all have the same diagonal sum. We prove a structure theorem for them and use it to characterize the quantum RCDS Erdős channels. We give an explicit form, up to unitary equivalence, for the Erdős channels of Choi rank $2$, and further show that there are uncountably many unitary equivalence classes of Erdős channels unlike the classical case. We also characterize the Erdős channels among mixed Weyl-unitary channels of any Choi rank.
  For qubits, we prove that there are exactly four unitary equivalence classes of Erdős channels and they are represented by the identity channel and uniform averages of Pauli channels. Finally, restricting to the diagonal channels, our results recover the Marcus--Ree inequality, the structure of RCDS doubly stochastic matrices, and the characterization of Erdős.

</details>


### [227] [Stratification of the space of density matrices via invariant theory and Uhlmann's connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02773)
*Howy Jordan,Markus J. Pflaum,Gerd Rudolph*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文用不变量理论证明了密度矩阵空间按秩分解构成Whitney (b)-正则分层，并将Uhlmann联络几何化为分层向量丛上的分层联络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息文献中曾断言将n×n密度矩阵状态空间按等秩分解可得Whitney分层，但一直缺乏严格证明；同时Uhlmann联络（描述混合态绝热和乐、在纯态上退化为Berry联络）的分层几何处理尚不明确。

Method: 采用不变量理论（invariant theory）的方法，对矩阵代数的秩分解进行分层分析，证明其为Whitney意义下的(b)-正则分层；并将Uhlmann联络作为分层向量丛上的分层联络进行几何刻画；附录给出分层纤维丛与分层联络概念的自洽阐述。

Result: 证明了矩阵代数秩分解构成Whitney (b)-正则分层；展示了Uhlmann联络可几何地视为分层向量丛上的分层联络；附录提供了分层纤维丛与分层联络的自足论述。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了量子信息理论中秩分层严格证明的空白，并为混合态绝热holonomy提供了分层几何框架，附录引入的分层联络概念可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: The space of $n\times n$ density matrices is the full state space of an $n$-level quantum system. While it has been asserted in the literature on quantum information theory that decomposing this state space into equirank pieces yields a Whitney stratification, rigorous proofs have remained absent. Using methods from invariant theory, this work provides a proof that the rank decomposition of the matrix algebra constitutes a (b)-regular stratification in the sense of Whitney. Furthermore, we demonstrate how the Uhlmann connection - which describes the adiabatic holonomy of mixed states and reduces to the Berry connection on pure states - can be geometrically treated as a stratified connection on a stratified vector bundle. Finally, our appendix provides a self-contained exposition of stratified fiber bundles and the new concept of stratified connections, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [228] [The monatomic limit of polyatomic gases as concentration of the internal-energy measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03060)
*Tommaso Ruggeri*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文在理性扩展热力学框架下研究多原子气体向单原子极限的奇异行为，证明内态密度幂次分布下归一化平衡分布在D→3时集中于I=0，并给出狄拉克规定及其一阶修正的严格依据，同时讨论经典ET14、动态压力容许区间、熵边界角点以及相对论情形下的条件伽马分布与Synge能量修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理性扩展热力学中，多原子气体的单原子极限往往是奇异的，需要严格数学依据来解释传统上使用的狄拉克规定及初始数据相容性要求。

Method: 针对内态密度φ(I)=I^a、a=(D-5)/2，分析归一化平衡分布在D→3时的集中行为；在经典ET14中考察内模能量与平衡的偏差-3Π/2、正定性约束-satisfying -p<Π<(D-3)p/3及熵在Π=0处的边界角点；相对论情形下在固定动量下导出内能的条件伽马分布、本构积分、精确能量恒等式与Synge能量的一阶修正。

Result: 证明当D→3时归一化平衡分布集中于I=0，严格验证狄拉克规定及其一阶修正；得到Π的容许范围并指出负动态压力可容许但固定负值在指定总能量下不趋近单原子态，说明需要相容初始数据；相对论情形下得到条件伽马分布及精确能量恒等式和Synge能量一阶修正。

Conclusion: 该工作为理性扩展热力学中多原子气体单原子极限的奇异性和狄拉克规定提供了严格数学基础，并明确了经典与相对论情形下负动态压力、初始数据相容性及能量修正的约束。

Abstract: In Rational Extended Thermodynamics, the monatomic limit of polyatomic gases is often singular. For the internal-state density $φ(I)=I^a$, with $a=(D-5)/2$, we prove that the normalized equilibrium distribution of the molecular internal energy $I$ concentrates at $I=0$ as $D\to3$, providing a rigorous justification of the Dirac prescription and its first-order correction. In classical ET14, the internal-mode energy differs from equilibrium by $-3Π/2$; positivity yields $-p<Π<(D-3)p/3$, while the exact entropy develops a boundary corner at $Π=0$. Negative dynamic pressures remain admissible, but fixed negative values do not approach the monatomic state with the prescribed total energy. This explains the need for compatible initial data. In the relativistic case, the internal energy is conditionally gamma distributed at fixed momentum; the constitutive integrals follow directly, and we derive an exact energy identity and the first correction to the Synge energy.

</details>


### [229] [Conformal Bootstrap for surfaces with boundary in Liouville CFT. Part II: spectral resolution and bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03539)
*Colin Guillarmou,Rémi Rhodes,Baojun Wu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文是紧致带边曲面Liouville共形场论共形bootstrap证明的第二部分，建立半环带半群的谱理论并给出bootstrap公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作为Liouville CFT共形bootstrap证明的延续，需处理带边曲面情形，理解半环带半群的谱结构并导出边界情形下的bootstrap公式。

Method: 基于Part 1建立的Segal公理与粘合性质，用散射方法将半环带半群生成元等同于边界哈密顿量并作谱分解，得到边界态空间关于不可约Virasoro表示的直积分分解，结合边界形变的Ward恒等式。

Result: 给出边界态空间的直积分分解（由边界谱测度控制）；将一般关联函数表示为切割曲线上谱参数的积分，被积函数为体与边界结构常数及共形块的乘积。

Conclusion: 结果支撑共形块的解析性与对称性，并有助于在共形块空间上构造映射类群表示。

Abstract: This paper is the second part of the proof of the conformal bootstrap for Liouville conformal field theory on compact surfaces with boundary. It is devoted to the spectral theory of the half-annulus semigroup and to the resulting bootstrap formula. Building on the Segal axioms and gluing properties established in Part~1~\cite{GRW1}, we identify the generator of the half-annulus semigroup with the boundary Hamiltonian and establish its spectral decomposition by scattering methods. This yields a direct-integral decomposition of the boundary state space into irreducible Virasoro representations, governed by the boundary spectral measure. Combining this decomposition with Ward identities for boundary deformations, we express general correlation functions as integrals over spectral parameters attached to cutting curves, with integrands given by products of bulk and boundary structure constants and the corresponding conformal blocks. These results also underpin the analyticity and symmetry properties of conformal blocks~\cite{GRSSbloc}, and contribute to the construction of mapping class group representations on spaces of conformal blocks~\cite{PapierBlocs}.

</details>


### [230] [Virasoro Conformal Blocks and modular functor from Liouville CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03540)
*Guillaume Baverez,Colin Guillarmou,Antti Kupiainen,Rémi Rhodes,Yuxiao Xie*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在中心荷 c>25 时，对带 m 个标记点、亏格 g 的黎曼曲面的 Teichmüller 空间上的 Virasoro 共形块给出了整体构造，证明它们是全纯线丛的整体全纯截面，并满足 Ward 恒等式。对三点球面，共形块空间一维；对给定标记黎曼曲面与标记裤子分解，该空间为无限维 Hilbert 空间，同构于按 DOZZ 结构常数测度的 L²(R_+^{3g-3+m})，并承载映射类群的射影酉表示，且每个 Moore-Seiberg 变换给出酉同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Virasoro 共形块在模空间/Teichmüller 空间上的整体解析与函数空间结构，为共形场论与低维拓扑、几何之间的对应提供严格数学基础，特别是理解共形块对映射类群的作用及不同裤子分解下构型空间的转换关系。

Method: 针对 c>25，在 g 亏格、m 标记点的黎曼曲面 Teichmüller 空间上进行 Virasoro 共形块的整体构造；证明其为某全纯线丛的整体全纯截面并满足 Ward 恒等式；借助标记裤子分解，将共形块空间识别为关于 DOZZ 结构常数测度的 L²(R_+^{3g-3+m})；进一步分析其在 Moore-Seiberg 变换下的酉同构与映射类群的射影酉表示。

Result: 共形块可整体定义为全纯线丛的全纯截面并满足 Ward 恒等式；三点球面情形空间一维；带标记裤子分解的一般情形为无限维 Hilbert 空间，等距同构于 L²(R_+^{3g-3+m})，测度由 DOZZ 结构常数给出；该空间承载映射类群的射影酉表示，且每种 Moore-Seiberg 变换对应一个酉同构。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了 Virasoro 共形块在 Teichmüller 空间上的整体全纯构造与 Hilbert 空间刻画，统一了共形不变性（Ward 恒等式）、函数空间表示及映射类群/ Moore-Seiberg 变换的酉结构。

Abstract: In this article, we give a global construction of the Virasoro conformal blocks on Teichmüller space of Riemann surfaces of genus $g$ with $m$ marked points, when the central charge is $c>25$. We prove that they are global holomorphic sections of a holomorphic line bundle and they satisfy the Ward identity, which encodes their conformal invariance. For the sphere with $3$ points, the space of conformal blocks is one-dimensional. For a given marked Riemann surface and a marked pair of pants decomposition, it is in general an infinite dimensional Hilbert space, isomorphic to $L^2(\R_+^{3g-3+m})$ with respect to some measure involving the DOZZ structure constants. We show that it carries a projective unitary representation of the mapping class group. More generally, there are unitary isomorphisms for each Moore-Seiberg move, changing a marked pair of pants decomposition into another one.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Parabolic induction for Takiff algebras and character formulas in Chari's category $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02787)
*Fulin Chen,Xin Huang,Ruling Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文引入Takiff代数$\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$的抛物子代数概念，用单根系的$(N+2)$-划分对其分类，并给出抛物诱导模不可约性的充要判据（推广Jantzen判据），进而导出仿射Lie代数上Chari范畴$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}$中几乎全部不可约Harish-Chandra模的特征标公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有理论中，有限维复半单Lie代数的抛物子代数分类及抛物诱导模的Jantzen不可约性判据已成熟，而Takiff代数作为其推广（$\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$）的相关结构理论尚不完善；同时仿射Lie代数上Chari范畴$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}$中Harish-Chandra模的特征标公式仅部分已知，亟需更系统的刻画工具。

Method: 1) 定义$\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}=\mathfrak{g}\otimes(\mathbb{C}[t]/t^{N+1}\mathbb{C}[t])$的抛物子代数；2) 用$\mathfrak{g}$单根系的$(N+2)$-划分对这些抛物子代数进行分类；3) 研究相应的抛物诱导$\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$-模，建立其不可约性的充要判据，作为Jantzen判据的推广；4) 将该判据应用于Chari范畴$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}$中Harish-Chandra模的特征标计算。

Result: 给出$\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$抛物子代数的$(N+2)$-划分分类；建立抛物诱导$\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$-模不可约性的充要判据，推广了Jantzen判据；导出与$\mathfrak{g}$相伴（无心）仿射Lie代数上Chari范畴$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}$中几乎全部不可约Harish-Chandra模的特征标公式；对$\mathfrak{g}=\mathfrak{sl}_3(\mathbb{C})$情形给出该范畴中所有不可约Harish-Chandra模的显式特征标公式。

Conclusion: Takiff代数的抛物子代数分类与抛物诱导模不可约性判据为研究仿射Lie代数表示提供了有效框架，成功推广了经典Jantzen判据并推动Chari范畴$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}$特征标理论的发展；$\mathfrak{sl}_3$的完整情形验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak{g}$ be a finite-dimensional complex semisimple Lie algebra, and let $\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}=\mathfrak{g}\otimes (\mathbb{C}[t]/t^{N+1}\mathbb{C}[t])$ denote the $N$-th Takiff algebra of $\mathfrak{g}$. We introduce a notion of parabolic subalgebras of $\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$ and classify them in terms of $(N+2)$-partitions of a simple root system of $\mathfrak{g}$. We then study the corresponding parabolically induced $\mathcal{C}_N\mathfrak{g}$-modules and establish a necessary and sufficient criterion for their irreducibility, extending Jantzen's criterion for parabolically induced $\mathfrak{g}$-modules. As an application, we derive character formulas for almost all irreducible Harish-Chandra modules in Chari's category $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}$ over the (centerless) affine Lie algebra associated with $\mathfrak{g}$. In particular, when $\mathfrak{g}=\mathfrak{sl}_3(\mathbb{C})$, we obtain explicit character formulas for all irreducible Harish-Chandra modules in this category.

</details>


### [232] [Maximal graded subalgebras of simple vectorial Lie superalgebras with polynomial coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03340)
*Dimitry Leites,Irina Shchepochkina*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在带有多项式系数的单（或接近单的Cartan prolongation）向量李超代数中，借助Weisfeiler滤过诱导的分次结构，分类了极大的单（及接近单的）分次子代数；结合Shchepochkina关于矩阵李超代数极大子代数的结果，解决了Dynkin问题的超化与无穷维版本（3个例外环境除外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S. Lie最早尝试分类多项式系数单向量李代数的极大子代数，但其附录表明该问题在一般意义下是wild的：描述所有子代数包含矩阵对在同时共轭下的分类，子代数过多。因此需要限定重要子类。本文旨在将Lie的问题超化，并在无穷维向量李超代数框架下解决Dynkin问题的超版本与无穷维版本。

Method: 研究对象为带多项式系数的单（或接近单的Cartan prolongation）向量李超代数，采用与Weisfeiler滤过相关联的分次结构，对极大的单（及接近单的）分次子代数进行描述与分类；并与I. Shchepochkina对矩阵李超代数极大子代数的工作相结合。

Result: 给出了单（或接近单）向量李超代数中极大单（及接近单）分次子代数的描述，并据此完成了Dynkin问题在超情形与无穷维情形下的求解，仅剩3个例外环境另行处理。

Conclusion: 该工作与Shchepochkina的结果共同解决了Dynkin问题（单有限维李代数极大子代数的描述）的超化与无穷维推广，除3个例外环境外。

Abstract: S. Lie was the first to try to classify maximal subalgebras of simple vectorial (i.e., consisting of vector fields) Lie algebras with polynomial coefficients. In Appendix it is shown that, as stated, the problem is wild: describing all subalgebras contains the classification of pairs of matrices up to simultaneous conjugation, so there are too many such subalgebras. Several researchers distinguished various classes of maximal subalgebras important in applications.
  Here, in simple (or in Cartan prolongations close to simple) vectorial Lie superalgebras with polynomial coefficients considered with gradings associated with Weisfeiler filtrations, we describe maximal simple (and close to simple) graded subalgebras --- a superization of what Sophus Lie tackled.
  Together with I. Shchepochkina's description of the maximal subalgebras of matrix Lie superalgebras, see arXiv:hep-th/9702122, this paper solves the super and infinite-dimensional version of Dynkin's problem --- the description of the maximal subalgebras of simple finite-dimensional Lie algebras --- except for the 3 exceptional ambients considered elsewhere.

</details>


### [233] [Maximal subalgebras in the three exceptional simple finite-dimensional Lie superalgebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03354)
*Dimitry Leites,Oleksandr Lozhechnyk*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文解决了 $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$、$\mathfrak{ag}(2)$、$\mathfrak{ab}(3)$ 这三个例外情形的分级子代数分类问题，给出所有 $\mathbb{Z}$-分级下的极大分级子代数，并提供升级为极大子代数的判据，同时发现第六种分级子代数类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dynkin 分类了有限维单李代数的极大子代数，Shchepochkina 及 Leites-Shchepochkina 将超版本推广到矩阵李超代数和具有多项式系数的单向量李超代数，但遗留了 $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$、$\mathfrak{ag}(2)$、$\mathfrak{ab}(3)$ 三个例外，因为它们以多种方式向量化，不被一般论证覆盖。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对三个李超代数在分级子代数框架下，枚举其所有 $\mathbb{Z}$-分级（$\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$ 两个、$\mathfrak{ag}(2)$ 五个、$\mathfrak{ab}(3)$ 八个），在每一个分级下描述所有极大分级子代数，半单部分显式给出，并给出把“极大分级”升级为“极大”的简单判据。全部计算在 $\mathbb{Q}$ 上完成。

Result: 完整给出了三个李超代数在各自所有适用 $\mathbb{Z}$-分级下的极大分级子代数列表，并描述其半单部分；通过判据将多数结论从极大分级升级为极大。副产品：发现向量李超代数分级子代数的经典五类划分在深度 $>1$ 时不完全，需补充第六类，且该第六类恰在此处非空；同时内蕴计算了 $\mathfrak{ag}(2)$、$\mathfrak{ab}(3)$ 内 $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$ 副本的参数 $a$。

Conclusion: 本文完成了三个例外李超代数 $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$、$\mathfrak{ag}(2)$、$\mathfrak{ab}(3)$ 的极大分级子代数分类，修正了向量李超代数分级子代数的分类框架（新增第六类），并给出参数 $a$ 的内蕴计算，从而补充完善了 Dynkin 问题的超版本。

Abstract: Dynkin classified the maximal subalgebras of the simple finite-dimensional Lie algebras. The super version of his problem is solved, for the matrix Lie superalgebras, by Shchepochkina, and, for the simple vectorial ones with polynomial coefficients taken with their Weisfeiler gradings, in the companion paper by Leites and Shchepochkina --- with three exceptions left open: the three simple finite-dimensional Lie superalgebras $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$, $\mathfrak{ag}(2)$ and $\mathfrak{ab}(3)$, which are vectorial in more than one way and are not covered by the general argument.
  Here we settle these three cases for \textbf{graded} subalgebras. For each of the three Lie superalgebras, we list all the $\mathbb{Z}$-gradings to which the method of that paper applies --- two for $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$, five for $\mathfrak{ag}(2)$, eight for $\mathfrak{ab}(3)$ --- and describe, for every one of them, all the maximal graded subalgebras, the semi-simple ones explicitly. A simple criterion upgrades most of the answers from ``maximal graded\rq\rq\ to ``maximal\rq\rq. Every answer is computed over $\mathbb{Q}$.
  Two by-products: the classical list of \textbf{five} cases into which the graded subalgebras of a vectorial Lie superalgebra split is incomplete for ambients of depth $>1$ --- a sixth case has to be added, and it is non-empty precisely here; and the parameter $a$ of the copies of $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$ sitting inside $\mathfrak{ag}(2)$, resp. $\mathfrak{ab}(3)$, is computed intrinsically.

</details>

# math.SG [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [Remarks on periodic points, flux, and the mean index in symplectic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02539)
*Marcelo S. Atallah,Marta Batoréo,Brayan Ferreira*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 在闭的辛Calabi–Yau流形上，端点非退化的辛同痕在其通量相关的Morse–Novikov同调满足简单非消没条件时，具有任意大周期的简单周期点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非Hamilton辛同痕（辛同痕而非Hamilton微分同胚）的周期点存在性，这类问题的Floer理论设置比经典Hamilton情形更复杂，需要新的指标估计工具。

Method: 结合两项技术：(1) 在平均指标为偶整数的假设下，给出Conley–Zehnder指标的精确（sharp）估计；(2) 在非Hamilton的Floer理论框架中运用经典的渐近迭代论证。

Result: 证明了在通量相关Morse–Novikov同调满足非消没条件时，存在任意大周期的简单周期点；并将该指标估计应用于标准辛4维环面的辛动力学。

Conclusion: 为闭辛Calabi–Yau流形上的非Hamilton辛同痕提供了周期点存在性判据，并展示了Conley–Zehnder指标估计对具体辛动力学问题的适用性。

Abstract: It is shown that a symplectic isotopy with non-degenerate end-point on a closed symplectically Calabi--Yau manifold has simple periodic points of arbitrarily large period under a simple non-vanishing condition on the Morse--Novikov homology associated with its flux. The proof combines a sharp estimate for the Conley--Zehnder index under the assumption that the mean index is an even integer with a classical asymptotic iteration argument in the non-Hamiltonian Floer-theoretic setting. We also apply this estimate to the symplectic dynamics of the standard symplectic $4$-torus.

</details>


### [235] [Two degenerate intersections of Clifford tori in \(S^2\times S^2\) and \(\CP^2\)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02965)
*Wenmin Gong*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 在单调流形 S²×S² 与 CP² 上构造光滑时间周期哈密顿量，使得 Clifford torus 与其哈密顿像恰好交于两个孤立的退化点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单调流形上哈密顿系统与辛拓扑中拉格朗日子流形相交问题。Clifford torus 作为典型拉格朗日环面，其与哈密顿像的交点数目及性质（非横截/退化）是 Arnold 猜想与 Arnold chord 猜想相关的核心问题；本工作意在刻画最小相交情形下退化交点能否出现。

Method: 通过在 S²×S² 和 CP² 上显式构造光滑的时间周期哈密顿量（Hamiltonian），控制其时间-1 映射（Hamiltonian 像）与 Clifford torus 的相交结构，使交点数为两个且均为孤立退化点。具体构造技巧（生成函数、Morse 理论或几乎复结构方法）在摘要中未给出。

Result: 存在这样的光滑时间周期哈密顿量：其哈密顿像与 Clifford torus 的交集恰由两个孤立退化点构成。

Conclusion: 在单调流形 S²×S² 与 CP² 上，Clifford torus 与哈密顿像可以在最小数目下相交，且交点可为孤立退化点，表明退化相交并非仅由非单调性或扰动导致，而与流形结构相关。

Abstract: We construct smooth time-periodic Hamiltonians on the monotone manifolds \(S^2\times S^2\) and \(\CP^2\) for which the Clifford torus meets its Hamiltonian image in exactly two isolated degenerate points.

</details>
